Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The microevolution variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 434 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 494 and intersection form of rank 5.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the microevolution decomposition. By minimality, the element a^434 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 494-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
